<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>The decision to stop building things and simply take the money instead. A mature economy's admission that gold is more flexible than stone.
[END]
[CONCEPT_CITY_HISTORICAL]
<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>A settlement large enough that most of its inhabitants do not grow their own food. Every consequence of civilisation follows from that one fact.
[END]
[CONCEPT_CLASSIC_AGE_GAMEPLAY]
<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>The second age. Writing, law and organised warfare arrive together; cities begin to support specialists, and the first wonders become buildable.
[END]
[CONCEPT_COMMERCE_HISTORICAL]
<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>Exchange between strangers, conducted on trust in a system rather than trust in a person. The whole of urban wealth rests on it.
[END]
[CONCEPT_COMPUTER_AGE_GAMEPLAY]
<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>Information becomes the limiting resource. Science and commerce multipliers matter more than raw production, and stealth and espionage units come into their own.
[END]
[CONCEPT_CRIME_HISTORICAL]
<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>What happens in the gap between what a state forbids and what it can observe. The gap widens with distance from the capital.
[END]
[CONCEPT_DEFENSE_HISTORICAL]
<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>The older and cheaper half of warfare. It is generally underfunded in peace and frantically overfunded the season after a raid.
[END]
[CONCEPT_DIAMOND_AGE_GAMEPLAY]
<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>Late-game engineering. Terraforming, extreme improvements and the most expensive wonders in the game all become reachable here.
[END]
[CONCEPT_DIPLOMACY_HISTORICAL]
<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>The practice of getting what an army would take, without the army. Cheaper, slower, and reversible.
[END]
[CONCEPT_DISBAND_CITY_HISTORICAL]
<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>Unmaking a settlement deliberately. Rare, drastic, and almost always a decision about a frontier that could not be held.
[END]
[CONCEPT_EMBARGO_HISTORICAL]
<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>War conducted through the ledger. It hurts slowly, indiscriminately, and often the wrong people.
[END]
[CONCEPT_EMPIRE_SIZE_HISTORICAL]
<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>Every realm has a distance beyond which orders arrive late and taxes arrive short. Empires do not usually fall; they exceed this radius.
[END]
[CONCEPT_FOOD_HISTORICAL]
<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>The first constraint and the last one. No policy, army or wonder survives a people that is not eating.
[END]
[CONCEPT_FUTURE_AGE_GAMEPLAY]
<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>The final age. The advance tree is effectively exhausted and further research yields incremental Future Technology rather than new capabilities.
[END]
[CONCEPT_GOLD_HISTORICAL]
<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>Stored, portable agreement. Its usefulness is that it can become almost anything else on short notice.
[END]
[CONCEPT_GOODS_HISTORICAL]
<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>Things worth carrying a long way, which is a much shorter list than things worth having.

[CONCEPT_GOVERNMENTS_HISTORICAL]
<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>The arrangement a people accepts for deciding who may compel whom. Every form is a different trade between order and consent.
[END]
[CONCEPT_GROWTH_HISTORICAL]
<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>Populations expand to whatever their food, water and patience allow. Managing that ceiling is most of what governing a city is.
[END]
[CONCEPT_HAPPINESS_HISTORICAL]
<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>The measure of whether a population consents to be governed. It is cheap to maintain and enormously expensive to restore.
[END]
[CONCEPT_IMPROVEMENTS_HISTORICAL]
<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>Permanent works inside a city that change what it is capable of. Their cost is paid once and their upkeep forever.
[END]
[CONCEPT_INDUSTRIAL_AGE_GAMEPLAY]
<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>Production dominates. Output per city climbs steeply, and pollution and unhappiness become live constraints rather than background noise.

[CONCEPT_INFRASTRUCTURE_HISTORICAL]
<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>The works nobody notices until they fail: roads, drains, aqueducts, walls. Civilisation is largely maintenance.

[CONCEPT_MAINTENANCE_COST_HISTORICAL]
<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>Everything built must be kept. The standing cost of past decisions is what constrains present ones.
[END]
[CONCEPT_MEDIEVAL_AGE_GAMEPLAY]
<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>The age of walls, guilds and cavalry. Defensive improvements outpace attack, so wars become sieges and economies become the deciding factor.
[END]
[CONCEPT_MILITARY_READINESS_HISTORICAL]
<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>How much of a realm's substance is committed to the possibility of war. Too little invites it; too much causes it.

[CONCEPT_OFFER_HISTORICAL]
<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>A proposal made in the expectation of refusal, so that the refusal can be pointed to later.

[CONCEPT_OVERCROWDING_HISTORICAL]

[CONCEPT_POLLUTION_HISTORICAL]
<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>The cost of production that is paid by whoever lives downstream, which is why it took so long to be counted at all.

[CONCEPT_POPULATION_HISTORICAL]
<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>The one resource that produces every other one, and the only one with an opinion about how it is used.

[CONCEPT_PUBLIC_WORKS_HISTORICAL]

[CONCEPT_RATIONS_HISTORICAL]

[CONCEPT_REGARD_HISTORICAL]

<c:245,240,225><h:0,0,0>Asking, in circumstances where both parties understand what follows a refusal.

[TERRAIN_WATER_SHELF_HISTORICAL]
